Marketing and product surfaces default to the dark theme; transactional surfaces (buy crypto, deposit, exchange) flip to a light theme that shares the same yellow CTAs and gray-blue hairlines. href=\"#overview\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBinance reads like a financial trading platform that wants to feel both authoritative and energetic. The base atmosphere is \u003cstrong\u003edeep near-black canvas\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #0b0e11) holding white type and a single, ubiquitous accent: \u003cstrong\u003eBinance Yellow\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e — #FCD535). That yellow does almost all of the brand's heavy lifting — it carries every primary CTA, every value-claim headline (\"FUNDS ARE SAFU\"), every \"Sign Up\" pill, every featured tier indicator, and the wordmark itself. There is no secondary brand color. The system trusts the yellow voltage to do the brand work, and it carries it.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eType runs Binance's custom \u003cstrong\u003eBinanceNova\u003c/strong\u003e (display + body) and \u003cstrong\u003eBinancePlex\u003c/strong\u003e (numerical / financial display) stack. BinanceNova carries display headlines, section titles, and body copy. BinancePlex appears on price tickers, large stat numbers (transaction volumes, user counts, prize pools) — anywhere a number wants to feel \"tabular and reliable.\" Both run at modest weights — display sizes use weight 600-700 (bolder than typical marketing because trading platforms need numbers to read at a glance), body stays at 400.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe product is \u003cstrong\u003emulti-theme\u003c/strong\u003e: marketing surfaces (homepage, smart-money, futures arena) default to dark, while transactional surfaces (buy crypto, deposit, withdraw) flip to a light theme. The same yellow CTAs and gray-blue hairlines (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #eaecef) thread through both — only canvas, surface, and text tones flip. Trading \u003cstrong\u003egreen\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e — #0ecb81) and \u003cstrong\u003ered\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e — #f6465d) signal price direction in tables, charts, and price tickers across both modes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKey Characteristics:\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSingle accent color: \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e (#FCD535) does all brand voltage — primary CTAs, hero headlines, brand mark, badges. Used scarcely on dark for emphasis, ubiquitously on transactional dialogs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCustom type stack: \u003ccode\u003eBinanceNova\u003c/code\u003e (display + body) and \u003ccode\u003eBinancePlex\u003c/code\u003e (numbers, prices, financial data). Big stat numbers always render in BinancePlex for tabular consistency.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMulti-theme: marketing pages default dark (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e); transactional pages flip light (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e). Yellow CTAs and trading green/red are shared across both.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLight footer on dark body: the homepage uses \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-soft-light}\u003c/code\u003e (#fafafa) for the footer even when the body above it is dark — a deliberate inversion that visually closes the page.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTrading semantics: green up / red down (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e) for price changes, applied as text color rather than badge background.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCard surfaces: \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e (#1e2329) for elevated cards on dark; \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e for cards on light. No gradient surfaces, no atmospheric backdrops — flat color blocks throughout.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBorder radius is small to medium: \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.md}\u003c/code\u003e (6px) for primary buttons, \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (8px) for inputs and content cards, \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.xl}\u003c/code\u003e (12px) for elevated card containers, \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.pill}\u003c/code\u003e for prominent feature CTAs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSpacing follows a 4-multiple scale; major editorial bands sit at \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e (80px) — slightly tighter than typical marketing-only sites because product pages need denser layouts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eColors\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-colors\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Colors\" href=\"#colors\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eBrand \u0026amp; Accent\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-brand--accent\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Brand \u0026amp; Accent\" href=\"#brand--accent\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBinance Yellow\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e — #FCD535): The single brand color. Used for primary CTA backgrounds, the wordmark, brand-claim headlines (\"FUNDS ARE SAFU\"), trust badges (\"No.1 Trading Volume\"), large stat numbers in \u003ccode\u003e{component.stat-callout-card}\u003c/code\u003e, and inline links.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBinance Yellow Active\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary-active}\u003c/code\u003e — #f0b90b): The press / hover-darker variant. Slightly more saturated yellow.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBinance Yellow Disabled\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary-disabled}\u003c/code\u003e — #3a3a1f): A desaturated dark-yellow used on disabled CTAs over dark canvas.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAccent Turquoise\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.accent-turquoise}\u003c/code\u003e — #2dbdb6): A small secondary accent used very sparingly on Smart Money's \"Check Now\" CTA over dark surfaces. Treat as a single-product accent, not a system color.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSurface\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-surface\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Surface\" href=\"#surface\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe system has two canvas modes that map to product context:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDark mode (marketing default):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCanvas Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #0b0e11): The primary page floor. Near-black with a slight warm tint — never pure black.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSurface Card Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #1e2329): Cards, navigation dropdowns, secondary buttons over dark canvas, markets table.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSurface Elevated Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-elevated-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #2b3139): One step lighter, used for nested cards, hovered nav items, and chart background panels.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLight mode (transactional):\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCanvas Light\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #ffffff): The page floor on transactional pages (buy crypto, deposit forms, account dialogs).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSurface Soft Light\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-soft-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #fafafa): Footer surface and disabled states.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSurface Strong Light\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-strong-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #f5f5f5): Form input backgrounds in muted contexts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eHairlines \u0026amp; Borders\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-hairlines--borders\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Hairlines \u0026amp; Borders\" href=\"#hairlines--borders\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHairline on Light\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #eaecef): The 1px border tone on light surfaces. Dembrandt's frequency analysis confirms this as the highest-count token (1022 occurrences) — Binance uses hairlines liberally.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eHairline on Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #2b3139): The 1px border tone on dark surfaces. Same hex as \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-elevated-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — borders feel like surface steps, not ink lines.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBorder Strong\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.border-strong}\u003c/code\u003e — #cdd1d6): A heavier border tone used on disabled secondary buttons.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eText\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-text\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Text\" href=\"#text\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInk\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.ink}\u003c/code\u003e — #181a20): The strongest text on light surfaces. Display headlines on transactional pages.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBody on Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.body}\u003c/code\u003e — #eaecef): Default running-text on dark canvas — deliberately not pure white, slightly cooler.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eBody on Light\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.body-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e — #181a20): Same as ink — light-mode body text reuses the ink token.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMuted\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.muted}\u003c/code\u003e — #707a8a): Footer links, breadcrumbs, captions, table column headers. Works on both light and dark canvas.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMuted Strong\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.muted-strong}\u003c/code\u003e — #929aa5): A second-tier muted for emphasized labels.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOn Primary\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-primary}\u003c/code\u003e — #181a20): Black text on yellow primary CTAs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOn Dark\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e — #ffffff): Pure white for high-contrast headlines on dark canvas.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eTrading Semantics\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-trading-semantics\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Trading Semantics\" href=\"#trading-semantics\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTrading Up\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e — #0ecb81): Price-up green, used as text color in tables, charts, and inline ticker arrows. Never as a button background.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTrading Down\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e — #f6465d): Price-down red. Same usage rules as trading-up.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInfo / Focus\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-info--focus\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Info / Focus\" href=\"#info--focus\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInfo\u003c/strong\u003e (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.info}\u003c/code\u003e — #3b82f6): Inline info badges and the focus-ring base. The Tailwind \u003ccode\u003e--tw-ring-color\u003c/code\u003e token surfaced by dembrandt — used on input focus.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFont Family\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-font-family\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Font Family\" href=\"#font-family\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe system runs \u003cstrong\u003eBinanceNova\u003c/strong\u003e for display and body, and \u003cstrong\u003eBinancePlex\u003c/strong\u003e for numerical / financial data. Both are licensed Binance custom typefaces. The fallback stack walks \u003ccode\u003e-apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", Roboto, sans-serif\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe split is functional, not decorative:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBinanceNova → editorial type (headlines, paragraphs, button labels, nav)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBinancePlex → tabular numerical type (prices, volumes, percentages, stat counters, prize pools)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eMixing them is not optional — BinanceNova on a price ticker would lose the trading-platform character; BinancePlex on a paragraph would feel monospace-cold.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDisplay sizes use weight 700 — heavier than most marketing systems. This makes sense for a trading platform: numbers need to read at a glance, headlines need to compete with chart visualizations and dense data tables. The system will not soften display weight to 400 the way Airtable or Stripe does.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ccode\u003e{typography.number-display}\u003c/code\u003e and the smaller number variants always use \u003cstrong\u003eBinancePlex\u003c/strong\u003e, even when surrounding body type uses BinanceNova. Prices, volumes, and stat counters render in BinancePlex regardless of context — it is the system's \"trustworthy number\" voice.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eNote on Font Substitutes\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-note-on-font-substitutes\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Note on Font Substitutes\" href=\"#note-on-font-substitutes\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIf BinanceNova and BinancePlex are unavailable, \u003cstrong\u003eInter\u003c/strong\u003e is the closest open-source substitute for BinanceNova and \u003cstrong\u003eJetBrains Mono\u003c/strong\u003e or \u003cstrong\u003eIBM Plex Sans\u003c/strong\u003e is the closest substitute for BinancePlex (depending on whether tabular monospace fidelity matters more than humanist proportions). 4px.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTokens:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xxs}\u003c/code\u003e 4px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xs}\u003c/code\u003e 8px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.sm}\u003c/code\u003e 12px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.md}\u003c/code\u003e 16px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e 24px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xl}\u003c/code\u003e 32px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xxl}\u003c/code\u003e 48px · \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e 80px.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSection padding (vertical):\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e (80px) — slightly tighter than airy marketing sites (96px) because Binance pages mix marketing bands with dense product surfaces (markets tables, FAQ accordions).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCard internal padding:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (24px) for content cards and markets tables; \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xl}\u003c/code\u003e (32px) for QR-promo cards and CTA bands; \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.md}\u003c/code\u003e (16px) for trust badges and table rows.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGutters:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (24px) between cards in 3-up grids; \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.md}\u003c/code\u003e (16px) inside footer column gutters and dense FAQ lists.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eGrid \u0026amp; dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMax content width:\u003c/strong\u003e ~1280px centered on marketing pages; ~1440px on product surfaces (markets, smart-money tables) where horizontal density matters.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEditorial body:\u003c/strong\u003e Single 12-column grid; product pages often use 8/4 split (main panel + side rail).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eMarkets table:\u003c/strong\u003e 5-column header (Pair / Last Price / 24h Change / 24h Volume / Action), with the first column carrying coin icon + symbol pair.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFooter:\u003c/strong\u003e 6-column link list at desktop, wrapping to 2-up at tablet and 1-up on mobile.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhitespace denser than typical marketing sites — long-scroll pages mix hero bands with markets tables, FAQ accordions, and feature grids without much breathing room between them. The system trusts contrast (yellow vs. dark canvas, green vs. red price cells) to do the visual separation work, not whitespace. Where whitespace appears, it's always uniform — \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e between every major band.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" shadow, no border\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eBody sections, top nav, hero bands, footer\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSoft hairline\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e1px \u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInputs, table dividers, FAQ row separators, secondary buttons\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eCard surface\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e background on dark canvas, \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e on light context — no shadow\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eAll elevated cards (markets-table-card, QR-promo-card, feature-photo-card, trust-badges)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSubtle drop shadow\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFaint shadow visible only when a card sits over imagery\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eUsed sparingly on the buy-crypto-amount-card on transactional pages\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFocus ring\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u003ccode\u003e0 0 0 2px {colors.info-ring}\u003c/code\u003e at 50% alpha\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eInput + button keyboard focus state\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eThe elevation philosophy is \u003cstrong\u003eflat surfaces with color-block separation\u003c/strong\u003e. Binance does not use heavy drop shadows or glassmorphism — depth comes from the contrast between \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e (a 12-step lightness jump that reads as a clear elevation boundary).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDecorative Depth\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-decorative-depth\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Decorative Depth\" href=\"#decorative-depth\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eYellow → dark vertical gradient backdrop\u003c/strong\u003e on the Futures Arena hero: \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e fading down to \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e. This is a single-page treatment used for product-launch / event hero surfaces, not a system-wide signature.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCoin-stack illustrations\u003c/strong\u003e flanking large stat blocks (3D rendered crypto coins, trophy icons). icons, avatars\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eBinance's radius hierarchy is tighter than dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etop-nav-dark\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The marketing top nav on dark canvas. 64px tall, \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e background. Carries the yellow Binance wordmark at left, primary horizontal menu (Buy Crypto, Markets, Trade, Futures, Earn, Square, Smart Money, Campaigns), right-side cluster with language selector, light/dark toggle, \"Log In\" text link, \"Sign Up\" \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e. The wordmark uses \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e for \"BINANCE\" type.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etop-nav-light\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The transactional top nav on light canvas (buy crypto, deposit pages). Same layout but \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e background and \u003ccode\u003e{colors.ink}\u003c/code\u003e menu items.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eButtons\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-buttons\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Buttons\" href=\"#buttons\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-primary\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The signature primary CTA. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-primary}\u003c/code\u003e (black on yellow — the system's iconic combination), type \u003ccode\u003e{typography.button}\u003c/code\u003e, padding 12px × 24px, height 40px, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.md}\u003c/code\u003e (6px). Press state: \u003ccode\u003ebutton-primary-active\u003c/code\u003e darkens to \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary-active}\u003c/code\u003e (#f0b90b). Disabled state: \u003ccode\u003ebutton-primary-disabled\u003c/code\u003e desaturates to \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary-disabled}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-primary-pill\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A larger pill variant of the primary CTA used for top-of-page sign-up moments and product-launch heroes (Futures Arena \"Join Now\"). Same yellow + black combination, padding 14px × 32px, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.pill}\u003c/code\u003e (9999px). Use sparingly — the pill is a \"this is THE action\" signal.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-secondary-on-dark\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Used over \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e for less-emphasized actions. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.md}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-secondary-on-light\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Light-canvas equivalent. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e with \u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e 1px border, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.ink}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-tertiary-text\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Inline text button with no background. Used for \"Log In\" in the top nav and inline \"Read More\" links.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-trading-up\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A solid green button used on price-up signals (Buy / Long actions). Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.sm}\u003c/code\u003e (4px), padding 8px × 20px. Smaller and tighter than \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e because it appears in dense trading interfaces.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-trading-down\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Symmetric red variant for Sell / Short actions. Same shape, background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebutton-subscribe\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Compact yellow CTA used in the Smart Money traders table to subscribe to a top trader. Smaller height (28px) and tighter padding than the primary CTA — fits inside dense table rows. Same yellow + black combination.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etext-link\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Inline body links in \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e (yellow on dark, also yellow on light). No underline by default. Type inherits \u003ccode\u003e{typography.body-md}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCards \u0026amp; Containers\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-cards--containers\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Cards \u0026amp; Containers\" href=\"#cards--containers\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ehero-band-dark\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Full-width dark band carrying the homepage h1 + sub-headline + dual CTA pair. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e (80px). The h1 (\"316,258,026 USERS TRUST US\") uses \u003ccode\u003e{typography.hero-display}\u003c/code\u003e at 64px / 700 — the system's largest type role.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003estat-callout-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Inline yellow stat numbers (15,000 BTC, 7,488,223, $429,423,449). Transparent background, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e, type \u003ccode\u003e{typography.number-display}\u003c/code\u003e in BinancePlex. Used as a flat layout block, not a card with surface — the yellow text alone carries the visual weight.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etrust-badge\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Small dark cards holding \"No.1 Customer Service\" / \"No.1 Trading Volume\" claims. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (8px), padding 16px × 20px. Yellow numeric or word badge (\"No.1\") sits next to a short label.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003emarkets-table-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The right-side markets table on the homepage. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.xl}\u003c/code\u003e (12px), padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (24px). Carries a tab row (Popular / New listing / Top gainers), then a 5-column row of coin pairs with last price, 24h change %, action button. Each row uses \u003ccode\u003e{component.markets-row}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003emarkets-row\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A single row inside the markets table. Transparent background, 12px vertical padding, hairline divider between rows. Coin icon (32×32) + symbol on left; last price in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.number-md}\u003c/code\u003e (BinancePlex); 24h change cell colored by direction (\u003ccode\u003e{component.price-up-cell}\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e{component.price-down-cell}\u003c/code\u003e); right-aligned chevron icon for \"view detail.\"\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice-up-cell\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e / \u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice-down-cell\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Colored text cells for price changes. Transparent background, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e or \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e, type \u003ccode\u003e{typography.number-md}\u003c/code\u003e in BinancePlex. Always paired with a small triangle arrow indicating direction.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003efeature-photo-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"Trade on the go\" section's photo strip — 3 lifestyle photos showing people using the Binance app. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.xl}\u003c/code\u003e. Photos crop edge-to-edge, no internal padding around the image.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eqr-promo-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"Trade on the go. Anywhere, anytime.\" card with QR code. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.xl}\u003c/code\u003e, padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xl}\u003c/code\u003e (32px). Contains an h2 in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.title-md}\u003c/code\u003e, a body paragraph, app store badges (iOS / Android), and a centered QR code.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003efunds-safu-band\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The yellow-headlined \"FUNDS ARE SAFU\" band. Background stays \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, but the headline uses \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e at \u003ccode\u003e{typography.display-lg}\u003c/code\u003e. Below the headline, three large \u003ccode\u003e{component.stat-callout-card}\u003c/code\u003e numbers anchor the band: total BTC reserves, users helped, funds recovered.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003efaq-row\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A single FAQ accordion row. Transparent background, padding 20px vertical, hairline divider between rows. Closed state: question in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.title-sm}\u003c/code\u003e + chevron icon at right. Open state: question + answer body in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.body-md}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecta-band-dark\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"Secure, Low-Fee Trading on Binance\" pre-footer CTA band. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e (one step elevated from canvas), rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.xl}\u003c/code\u003e, padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.xxl}\u003c/code\u003e (48px). Carries an h2 in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.display-sm}\u003c/code\u003e and a \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e aligned right.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eLight-Mode Transactional Components\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-light-mode-transactional-components\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Light-Mode Transactional Components\" href=\"#light-mode-transactional-components\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ebuy-crypto-amount-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The right-rail card on the Buy BTC page. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (8px), padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (24px). Carries an editable amount input in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.number-display}\u003c/code\u003e (BinancePlex), a currency selector, and a yellow \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e for \"Continue\" / \"Confirm Order.\"\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003esteps-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"How to Buy Crypto\" 3-up cards (Enter Amount → Confirm Order → Receive Crypto). Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e, padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.lg}\u003c/code\u003e. Each card has a small numbered icon, a \u003ccode\u003e{typography.title-sm}\u003c/code\u003e step name, and a body description.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003eprice-chart-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"Bitcoin Markets\" card carrying the BTC price chart. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e. Top row carries pair selector ($79,065.04, +0.45%); main area is a candlestick / line chart in \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e; bottom row carries timeframe selector (24H / 1W / 1M / 3M / 1Y / ALL).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003econversion-cell\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A single row in the BTC ↔ USD conversion table. Transparent background, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.body-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e, type \u003ccode\u003e{typography.body-md}\u003c/code\u003e. Pair label on left (BTC, USDT, etc.); USD equivalent on right.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eInputs \u0026amp; Forms\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-inputs--forms\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Inputs \u0026amp; Forms\" href=\"#inputs--forms\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003esearch-input-on-dark\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The \"Search currencies\" input on the homepage hero. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-card-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.on-dark}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e (8px), padding 10px × 16px, height 40px. Carries a yellow \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary-pill}\u003c/code\u003e on the right side (\"Sign Up\").\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etext-input-on-light\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — Standard input on transactional pages. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e, 1px \u003ccode\u003e{colors.hairline-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e border, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.md}\u003c/code\u003e (6px), padding 10px × 16px, height 40px. Focus state inherits the focus-ring shadow.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecookie-consent-card\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The cookie banner card visible on the homepage. Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e, rounded \u003ccode\u003e{rounded.lg}\u003c/code\u003e, padding \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.md}\u003c/code\u003e (16px). Body text in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.body-sm}\u003c/code\u003e (13px / 400) with three stacked button options (Accept Cookies \u0026amp; Continue / Reject Additional Cookies / Manage Cookies).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSmart Money Sub-System\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-smart-money-sub-system\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Smart Money Sub-System\" href=\"#smart-money-sub-system\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003etrader-row\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — A single row in the top-traders table on /smart-money. Transparent background, padding 12px vertical, hairline divider between rows. Avatar + trader name + private/public badge on left; ROI %, AUM, mint date columns; yellow \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-subscribe}\u003c/code\u003e on right.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eSignature Components\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-signature-components\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Signature Components\" href=\"#signature-components\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003earena-hero-gradient\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The Futures Arena product-launch hero. A vertical gradient from \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e at top to \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e at bottom, with the prize-pool headline (4,000,000 USDT) in \u003ccode\u003e{typography.display-lg}\u003c/code\u003e centered. A \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary-pill}\u003c/code\u003e (\"Join Now\") sits below the headline. Used only on product-launch event surfaces — do not generalize to other heroes.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eFooter\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-footer\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Footer\" href=\"#footer\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003ccode\u003efooter-light\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e — The light-gray footer that closes every page (including dark-canvas pages). Background \u003ccode\u003e{colors.surface-soft-light}\u003c/code\u003e (#fafafa), text \u003ccode\u003e{colors.body-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e. 6-column link list at desktop covering Community / About Us / Products / Business / Service / Learn columns. Vertical padding 64px. The deliberate light footer on a dark page is one of Binance's most distinctive layout choices — it visually closes the page with a \"marketing reset\" surface.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDo's and Don'ts\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-dos-and-donts\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDo\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-do\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Do\" href=\"#do\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eReserve \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e (Binance Yellow) for primary actions, brand-claim headlines, and the wordmark. Never use it for secondary or decorative purposes — yellow's scarcity is what makes it powerful.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e (yellow with black text) as the universal primary CTA across both dark and light modes. The same button appears identically on \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-dark}\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e{colors.canvas-light}\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e (green) and \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e (red) only for explicit Buy/Sell or Long/Short actions. Never use them for general \"confirm\" or \"cancel\" because they carry semantic price-direction meaning.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse BinancePlex for every number. Prices, volumes, percentages, stat counters — all BinancePlex. Mixing BinanceNova into a number ticker breaks the trading-platform character.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eChoose canvas mode by surface intent: dark for marketing / product showcase / trading dashboards; light for transactional dialogs (buy / deposit / withdraw / form submission).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAnchor every editorial band with \u003ccode\u003e{spacing.section}\u003c/code\u003e (80px). Binance is denser than airy marketing sites — 80px is the right rhythm.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eDon't\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-dont\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Don't\" href=\"#dont\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't introduce a second brand color. The system has exactly one accent (\u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e) and any expansion dilutes the brand identity. The turquoise on Smart Money is a single-product experiment, not a system token.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't use yellow for body text or large surface fills. It is for focal-point CTAs and headlines only.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't use \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-up}\u003c/code\u003e / \u003ccode\u003e{colors.trading-down}\u003c/code\u003e as background fills on cards. They are price-direction signals, expressed as text color or small badge fill — never as a card surface.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't soften display weight. \u003ccode\u003e{typography.hero-display}\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e{typography.display-lg}\u003c/code\u003e are intentionally weight 700 — going to 400 reads as design-portfolio, not trading platform.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't add atmospheric gradients to the canvas (mesh, aurora, glow effects). Binance trusts color-block contrast — adding atmospheric depth muddies the trading-platform feel.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDon't invert \u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e's text color. Black on yellow is the system's signature — white text on yellow loses contrast and brand recognition.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 Changes\u003c/th\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/thead\u003e\n\u003ctbody\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eMobile\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u0026lt; 768px\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTop nav collapses to hamburger; hero h1 drops from 64px to ~36px; markets table converts to a horizontally-scrollable card list; demo grids drop to 1-up; footer 6 columns wrap to 2\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTablet\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e768–1024px\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eTop nav stays horizontal but tightens, secondary menu items hide behind a \"More\" dropdown; markets table 2-up; pricing/feature grids 2-up\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eDesktop\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e1024–1440px\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eFull top-nav with all primary menu items; 5-column markets table; trading dashboards in 8/4 split (chart + side rail)\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003ctr\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eWide\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003e\u0026gt; 1440px\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003ctd\u003eSame as desktop with more outer breathing room; max content width caps at 1280-1440px depending on surface\u003c/td\u003e\n\u003c/tr\u003e\n\u003c/tbody\u003e\n\u003c/table\u003e\u003c/markdown-accessiblity-table\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" (\u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e height + padding) — meets WCAG AAA's 44 × 44 with surrounding spacing.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSubscribe / inline action buttons are 28 × 28 — denser than ideal but matches industry trading platform norms.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCoin icons in markets tables are 32 × 32px, with the entire row tappable for 44px+ effective target.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eCollapsing Strategy\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-collapsing-strategy\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Collapsing Strategy\" href=\"#collapsing-strategy\"\u003e\u003csvg dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTop nav collapses to hamburger at \u0026lt; 768px; the menu opens as a full-screen sheet with the same yellow accent CTAs anchored to the bottom of the sheet.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMarkets table reflows to a horizontally-scrollable single card per coin pair on mobile.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe hero stat numbers (\"316M USERS\") shrink proportionally rather than wrapping — Binance's biggest claim must always read as a single block.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTrading dashboards switch from chart + side-rail to chart-only with a separate \"Trade\" tab on mobile.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe light footer stays full-bleed at every breakpoint — it does not collapse to a separate dark variant.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eImage 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCoin icons stay at fixed 24/32px sizes regardless of breakpoint.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLifestyle photos in the \"Trade on the go\" section crop responsively — wider at desktop, taller (vertical) at mobile.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e3D coin-stack illustrations are fixed-aspect-ratio assets that scale uniformly without cropping.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eIteration Guide\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-iteration-guide\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Iteration Guide\" href=\"#iteration-guide\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFocus on ONE component at a time. Reference its YAML key directly (\u003ccode\u003e{component.button-primary}\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e{component.markets-row}\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen adding a new component, decide first whether it lives in dark mode (marketing / product) or light mode (transactional). The same component appears in both with surface tone flipped.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eVariants of an existing component (\u003ccode\u003e-active\u003c/code\u003e, \u003ccode\u003e-disabled\u003c/code\u003e) live as separate entries in \u003ccode\u003ecomponents:\u003c/code\u003e — never as nested state objects.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse \u003ccode\u003e{token.refs}\u003c/code\u003e everywhere prose mentions a color, a radius, a typography role, or a spacing value.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNever document hover. The system documents Default and Active/Pressed states only.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eNumbers always use BinancePlex; copy always uses BinanceNova. Mixing them is a system violation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eTrading green / red are semantic price tokens — never repurpose them for \"success\" or \"error\" generic states.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eKnown Gaps\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-known-gaps\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: Known Gaps\" href=\"#known-gaps\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe dembrandt frequency analyzer captured \u003ccode\u003e#eaecef\u003c/code\u003e (light hairline, count 1022) as the highest-frequency token. The brand-defining \u003ccode\u003e{colors.primary}\u003c/code\u003e (#FCD535) appears far less frequently because it's used scarcely as accent — its system role had to be confirmed from screenshots.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBinanceNova and BinancePlex weight-axis values are not formalized as variable-font tokens — only the static weights observed in screenshots are documented.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAnimation and transition timings (chart redraws, price-change flashes) are not in scope.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eForm validation states beyond \u003ccode\u003e{component.text-input-on-light}\u003c/code\u003e defaults are not extracted — error / success input variants would need a sign-up or order-confirmation flow to confirm.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe trading dashboard surfaces (Spot / Futures / Margin) were not in the analyzed URL set; their order book, candlestick chart configuration, and position-management cards are not documented here.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eThe light/dark theme toggle behavior (whether transactional pages can be forced dark by user preference) is product behavior, not extracted from the marketing surfaces.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/article\u003e","richTextTruncated":false,"renderedFileInfo":null,"symbols":{"timed_out":false,"not_analyzed":false,"symbols":[{"name":"Overview","kind":"section_2","ident_start":9490,"ident_end":9498,"extent_start":9487,"extent_end":12760,"fully_qualified_name":"Overview","ident_utf16":{"start":{"line_number":333,"utf16_col":3},"end":{"line_number":333,"utf16_col":11}},"extent_utf16":{"start":{"line_number":333,"utf16_col":0},"end":{"line_number":351,"utf16_col":0}}},{"name":"Colors","kind":"section_2","ident_start":12763,"ident_end":12769,"extent_start":12760,"extent_end":16400,"fully_qualified_name":"Colors","ident_utf16":{"start":{"line_number":351,"utf16_col":3},"end":{"line_number":351,"utf16_col":9}},"extent_utf16":{"start":{"line_number":351,"utf16_col":0},"end":{"line_number":394,"utf16_col":0}}},{"name":"Brand
